# YT9215 registers in the vendor SDK table list

This file is generated by [gen_registers.py](gen_registers.py) from the machine-generated register table list in the Motorcomm YT9215 switch SDK. The overview is in [README.md](README.md).

The SDK list carries addresses, sizes and field positions but no descriptions, so the names are the only statement of meaning. The module name is the SDK module whose base address is the nearest one at or below the table address. Tables listed at address 0x000000 are the ACL key and mask layouts for each key type. The SDK gives them no register address, so they have no module.
